# Cultivation / Returner Slop — sources

300 tossups on Korean manhwa, webtoons and web novels, Chinese web novels, their shared tropes and
mechanics, and the reading ecosystem (platforms, translation and piracy sites, industry drama). Every
sentence of every question is listed below with at least one source that was opened during research
(September 2026).

How pages were read: fandom.com and some Wikipedia pages return a Cloudflare challenge to automated fetches,
so those pages were read through the same wiki's MediaWiki API (`api.php?action=parse` / `action=raw` /
`prop=extracts`) for the exact page listed; the canonical page URL is given. NovelUpdates, Namu Wiki and a few
Korean news pages were read through Wayback Machine copies where the live site blocked fetching. News and
publisher pages were opened directly.

## 1. Beru
- "Guarding Jinwoo's traumatized sister Jinah, got bored, watched Korean period dramas, developed a historic manner of speech": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/wiki/Beru
- "After Hwang Dongsoo nearly killed Jinho, Jinwoo used Shadow Exchange to swap places with Beru, who healed Jinho from the brink of death": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/wiki/Beru ; Hwang Dong-soo abducted and tortured Jin-ho: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Solo_Leveling
- "Named after French novelist Bernard Werber, author of Empire of the Ants": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/wiki/Beru (Jeju Island Arc section and Trivia)
- "The Ant King gained speech and healing magic by devouring S-rank healer Min Byung-gyu's head via Gluttony": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/wiki/Ant_King ; Min Byung-gyu is an S-rank healer: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Solo_Leveling
- "Hidden boss of the Jeju Island S-rank gate; killed Goto Ryuji, Japan's strongest hunter": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/wiki/Ant_King ; https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Solo_Leveling (Goto "the strongest hunter in Japan ... killed ... by the Ant King")
- "Marshal-grade, insect-type shadow extracted from the Ant King by Sung Jinwoo": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/wiki/Beru (infobox Type=Insect, Rank=Marshal Grade, Origin=Ant King) ; https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Solo_Leveling ("an ant shadow that was extracted from the Ant King ... later becomes a Marshal")

- "Tencent Video and iQIYI charged subscribers extra to watch episodes early in 2019; backlash; iQIYI later lost a lawsuit": https://www.scmp.com/abacus/tech/article/3042901/netflix-and-hulu-china-want-users-pay-watch-episodes-early ; https://www.scmp.com/tech/apps-social/article/3087424/netflix-style-service-iqiyi-ordered-pay-subscriber-us210-levying (Beijing Internet Court, June 2020); https://zh.wikipedia.org/wiki/庆余年 (VIP 超前点播 controversy section)

Questions 101–300 draw from series carried by Asura Scans. Rank references use the site's published top-30 comics ranking; titles outside that list were confirmed through Asura's live series pages and catalogue sitemap during research (September 2026): https://asurascans.com/series-ranking

Source series: Surviving the Game as a Barbarian (Asura rank 1) — Asura page: https://asurascans.com/comics/surviving-the-game-as-a-barbarian-3ec3b16f

- "It is reckoned one of the top three houses of healing in all of Jianghu, alongside Hwaju, whose teaching method is beatings." — https://doctors-rebirth.fandom.com/wiki/Hwaju_Clinic
- "Once expected to die out, it was revived by a transmigrated surgeon who founded and heads its surgery department." — https://doctors-rebirth.fandom.com/wiki/White_Qilin_Medical_Hall
- "For 10 points, name this institution of this murim series, famous for acupuncture and for its warriors' skill with the five-element techniques." — https://doctors-rebirth.fandom.com/wiki/White_Qilin_Medical_Hall

## 208. the Barony of Frontera
Source series: The Greatest Estate Developer (Asura rank 4) — Asura page: https://asurascans.com/comics/the-greatest-estate-developer-3ec3b16f

- "Waste from this place travels along an underground sewage canal to a sludge treatment facility, where Solitas incinerates it once a day." — https://dragonschef.fandom.com/wiki/Barony_of_Frontera
- "Its people may live in the Honeypot Apartments, condensed housing that becomes impenetrable once its main gates close." — https://dragonschef.fandom.com/wiki/Barony_of_Frontera
- "Its food comes largely from the Maritz Wetlands and from terrace farms, and its houses were fitted with ondol underfloor heating on its lord's instruction." — https://dragonschef.fandom.com/wiki/Barony_of_Frontera
- "Fuel for that heating comes from a black coal mine at the base of a nearby mountain." — https://dragonschef.fandom.com/wiki/Barony_of_Frontera
- "Lying in eastern Lorasia under Empress Alicia Termina Magentano, it was raised in rank in episode 66 for monumental feats of engineering." — https://dragonschef.fandom.com/wiki/Barony_of_Frontera
- "Its debt-ridden ruling family is fronted by a lazy, hard-drinking noble whose body has been taken over by a transmigrated civil engineering student." — https://dragonschef.fandom.com/wiki/The_Greatest_Estate_Developer
- "For 10 points, name this fief along the River Prona whose redevelopment drives this webtoon." — https://dragonschef.fandom.com/wiki/Barony_of_Frontera

## 209. Ppodong
Source series: The Greatest Estate Developer (Asura rank 4) — Asura page: https://asurascans.com/comics/the-greatest-estate-developer-3ec3b16f

- "His own user manual asks readers to give him lots of love." — https://dragonschef.fandom.com/wiki/Ppodong
- "He comforted a saddened fellow summon named Hamang whose abilities his master had doubted, and later copied one of his master's evil grins to bully a lazy bird into working." — https://dragonschef.fandom.com/wiki/Ppodong
- "He leapt into danger to save the knight Javier from being crushed by a gigatitan." — https://dragonschef.fandom.com/wiki/Ppodong
- "Red sunflower seeds let him swell from a few inches to over thirty feet." — https://dragonschef.fandom.com/wiki/Ppodong
- "He was the first creature his master ever summoned, and he doubles as a construction assistant who often sits on that master's shoulder." — https://dragonschef.fandom.com/wiki/Ppodong
- "He disappears once his master activates the power called “absolute”." — https://dragonschef.fandom.com/wiki/Ppodong
- "For 10 points, name this fluffy brown hamster who serves the transmigrated lord of this webtoon." — https://dragonschef.fandom.com/wiki/Ppodong

Source series: Nano Machine (Asura rank 5) — Asura page: https://asurascans.com/comics/nano-machine-3ec3b16f

- "Its rise turned a three-way balance among the forces of justice, the forces of evil and the demonic cult into a four-way deadlock." — https://nano-mashine.fandom.com/wiki/Blade_God_Six_Martial_Clan

- "It succeeds a western predecessor that an overlord of the forces of evil annihilated while trying to seize a Soul Shifting Technique rumored to grant immortality." — https://absolute-sword-sense.fandom.com/wiki/Blood_Cult
- "Its members revere power, but pursue strength through brutality, violence and cruelty." — https://absolute-sword-sense.fandom.com/wiki/Blood_Cult
- "Its leaders traditionally carry a sentient, possessed weapon forged by the smith Ou Yezi." — https://absolute-sword-sense.fandom.com/wiki/Blood_Demon_Sword
- "Baek Moo-young held its leadership fifteenth and Baek Hye-hyang seventeenth, and their shared lineage with its sixteenth leader later faded away." — https://absolute-sword-sense.fandom.com/wiki/Blood_Cult
- "That sixteenth leader is this series' protagonist, and the title he took is named for the red fluid this organization worships." — https://absolute-sword-sense.fandom.com/wiki/Blood_Cult

## 212. the Blood Demon Sword
Source series: Absolute Sword Sense (Asura rank 6) — Asura page: https://asurascans.com/comics/absolute-sword-sense-3ec3b16f

- "When it touched Ha Seong-woon and Baek Hye-hyang, it circulated their vital fluid to save their lives instead of harming them." — https://absolute-sword-sense.fandom.com/wiki/Blood_Demon_Sword
- "It took perverse pleasure in being licked by Seolbaek, who lacked the lineage it usually demands, and even let her carry on." — https://absolute-sword-sense.fandom.com/wiki/Blood_Demon_Sword
- "Forged by the legendary smith Ou Yezi, it is one of the five yokai blades." — https://absolute-sword-sense.fandom.com/wiki/Blood_Demon_Sword
- "Legend holds that it possesses whoever wields it and devours their soul." — https://absolute-sword-sense.fandom.com/wiki/Blood_Demon_Sword
- "After its owner's defeat in the Great War, the orthodox alliance claimed it as a war trophy." — https://absolute-sword-sense.fandom.com/wiki/Blood_Demon_Sword
- "It is the sacred relic and symbol of the leader of a fanatical crimson faction, and was carried by Baek Wol, by Baek Moo-young, and by this series' protagonist." — https://absolute-sword-sense.fandom.com/wiki/Blood_Demon_Sword
- "For 10 points, name this arrogant, talking weapon that cackles about how good the red stuff tastes." — https://absolute-sword-sense.fandom.com/wiki/Blood_Demon_Sword

## 213. the Runcandel Family
Source series: Swordmaster's Youngest Son (Asura rank 7) — Asura page: https://asurascans.com/comics/swordmasters-youngest-son-3ec3b16f

- "Its pureblood members inherit a Blessed Body, which lets them repeatedly perform a Decisive Move that would endanger an ordinary fighter's life." — https://swordmasters-youngest-son.fandom.com/wiki/Runcandel_Family
- "On average its members reach one star at thirteen, three stars at sixteen, and five stars at twenty." — https://swordmasters-youngest-son.fandom.com/wiki/Star_Rank
- "In a thousand years of its history, not one member has failed to reach one star by fourteen." — https://swordmasters-youngest-son.fandom.com/wiki/Star_Rank
- "It believes in combat, conquest and control, and it dominates half the world by force." — https://swordmasters-youngest-son.fandom.com/wiki/Runcandel_Family
- "It was truly a house of magic swordsmen, a fact erased by divine influence and by the Zipfel family's manipulation of history." — https://swordmasters-youngest-son.fandom.com/wiki/Runcandel_Family
- "For 10 points, name this patriarchal clan of swordmasters whose regressed fifty-sixth patriarch leads this webtoon." — https://swordmasters-youngest-son.fandom.com/wiki/Runcandel_Family

## 214. the Garden of Swords
Source series: Swordmaster's Youngest Son (Asura rank 7) — Asura page: https://asurascans.com/comics/swordmasters-youngest-son-3ec3b16f

- "It serves as the symbol of that household's dreams and honor." — https://swordmasters-youngest-son.fandom.com/wiki/Garden_of_Swords
- "It is the main house of a patriarchal clan whose purebloods are born with the Blessed Body." — https://swordmasters-youngest-son.fandom.com/wiki/Runcandel_Family

## 215. Murakan

- "His aliases include Shadow Dragon, and in his true form his black scales are set off by blue shadow flames along his head and back." — https://swordmasters-youngest-son.fandom.com/wiki/Murakan
- "He is over three thousand years old and has been weakened by a damaged heart." — https://swordmasters-youngest-son.fandom.com/wiki/Murakan
- "For 10 points, name this abrasive, boisterous black dragon who befriends the hero of this regression webtoon." — https://swordmasters-youngest-son.fandom.com/wiki/Murakan

## 216. the Huazhong Contest
Source series: Return of the Mount Hua Sect (Asura rank 8) — Asura page: https://asurascans.com/comics/return-of-the-mount-hua-sect-3ec3b16f

- "This recurring event was proposed by the richer of its two participants after a war, and began as a friendly gathering held once every five years." — https://return-of-the-mount-hua-sect.fandom.com/wiki/Zhongnan-Huashan_Contest
- "It later became a biennial competition between second- and third-grade disciples, staged as a social occasion so that elders would not escalate matters." — https://return-of-the-mount-hua-sect.fandom.com/wiki/Zhongnan-Huashan_Contest
- "The host sect has always held it, while the challenger quietly paid just enough that refusing to host became impossible." — https://return-of-the-mount-hua-sect.fandom.com/wiki/Zhongnan-Huashan_Contest
- "The challenger dominated the duels for a century and eventually announced it wished to abolish the tradition, which would publicly rank the host beneath it." — https://return-of-the-mount-hua-sect.fandom.com/wiki/Zhongnan-Huashan_Contest
- "In its final staging, the host's second-grade disciples lost ten bouts in a row before its third-grade disciples won ten in a row." — https://return-of-the-mount-hua-sect.fandom.com/wiki/Zhongnan-Huashan_Contest
- "A regressed sword saint then proposed a tiebreaker in the form of a war of attrition." — https://return-of-the-mount-hua-sect.fandom.com/wiki/Zhongnan-Huashan_Contest
- "For 10 points, name this friendly contest of swords between two declining sects of the same province in this murim series." — https://return-of-the-mount-hua-sect.fandom.com/wiki/Zhongnan-Huashan_Contest

- "It is packed with traps, so a visitor could fairly call the whole place a lethal prank." — https://return-of-the-mount-hua-sect.fandom.com/wiki/Sword_Crypt
- "A letter left inside grants its legacy to whoever grasps its true meaning and braves its deadly perils." — https://return-of-the-mount-hua-sect.fandom.com/wiki/Sword_Crypt
- "For 10 points, name this tomb of this murim series, whose real treasure turns out to be a healing pellet's formula." — https://return-of-the-mount-hua-sect.fandom.com/wiki/Sword_Crypt

Source series: Return of the Mount Hua Sect (Asura rank 8) — Asura page: https://asurascans.com/comics/return-of-the-mount-hua-sect-3ec3b16f

- "A letter accompanying its formula insists it be dedicated to saving the lives of countless people under the heavens." — https://return-of-the-mount-hua-sect.fandom.com/wiki/Sword_Crypt
- "Its known users include Yao Xian and the regressed swordsman who recovered that formula." — https://return-of-the-mount-hua-sect.fandom.com/wiki/Primordial_Panacea

## 221. the Cerberus Mercenary Corps

- "It caused so many problems and behaved so unpredictably that completing a request was nearly impossible, which earned it a canine nickname." — https://the-regressed-mercenarys-machinations.fandom.com/wiki/The_Cerberus_Mercenary_Corps
- "It stayed in business anyway, since its fighters individually matched knights-in-training and would use any means at all in combat." — https://the-regressed-mercenarys-machinations.fandom.com/wiki/The_Cerberus_Mercenary_Corps

## 222. Dungeon Babies
Source series: Dungeon Odyssey (Asura rank 12) — Asura page: https://asurascans.com/comics/dungeon-odyssey-3ec3b16f

- "They were born to humans who had been kidnapped by underground creatures and forced to live below the surface." — https://dungeon-odyssey.fandom.com/wiki/Dungeon_Babies
- "Humans grade them by the floor they were born on, so that those from floors one to three are only slightly stronger than an adult man and usually join exploration teams." — https://dungeon-odyssey.fandom.com/wiki/Dungeon_Babies
- "Those from floors four to six reach superhuman strength and fill out most of a raid's assault team, while those from floors seven to nine are highly respected." — https://dungeon-odyssey.fandom.com/wiki/Dungeon_Babies
- "Anyone born below the tenth floor has strength humans cannot even understand, since mana grows denser the deeper one goes." — https://dungeon-odyssey.fandom.com/wiki/Dungeon_Babies
- "All of them absorb and manipulate mana, which surrounds them with an aura and lets them adapt so thoroughly that they can digest rocks." — https://dungeon-odyssey.fandom.com/wiki/Dungeon_Babies
- "When the war ended, the labyrinth masters released them, and most of them fought their way up to the surface." — https://dungeon-odyssey.fandom.com/wiki/Dungeon_Babies
- "For 10 points, give this human nickname for the underground-born children of this manhwa, among them the protagonist Kim Jinwoo." — https://dungeon-odyssey.fandom.com/wiki/Dungeon_Odyssey

## 223. Geyser
Source series: Dungeon Odyssey (Asura rank 12) — Asura page: https://asurascans.com/comics/dungeon-odyssey-3ec3b16f

- "Paju holds six of the gates leading into it, lettered A through F, of which D, E and F are open to anyone who shows identification." — https://dungeon-odyssey.fandom.com/wiki/Geyser
- "Its floors below the tenth are collectively called the Abyss, and it is divided into at least twelve floors in all." — https://dungeon-odyssey.fandom.com/wiki/Geyser

- "Humans fought back and seized those entrances, which are now blocked by huge doors and guarded by armed forces that register everyone entering and leaving." — https://dungeon-odyssey.fandom.com/wiki/Geyser
- "By the end of the war humans had mapped and almost entirely cleared its first two floors." — https://dungeon-odyssey.fandom.com/wiki/Geyser
- "For 10 points, name this cavernous complex hundreds of meters below the planet's surface, the setting of this manhwa." — https://dungeon-odyssey.fandom.com/wiki/Geyser

## 225. the Great Regression Technique
Source series: Absolute Regression (Asura rank 13) — Asura page: https://asurascans.com/comics/absolute-regression-3ec3b16f

- "Five of the ninety-nine materials it requires were exactly the ones the Gwi family could never obtain." — https://absoluteregression.fandom.com/wiki/Great_Regression_Technique

- "The list also demands the core of a ten-thousand-year fire carp, a Secret Demon Soul, and five million nyang." — https://absoluteregression.fandom.com/wiki/Great_Regression_Technique
- "Researched for centuries and handed down in the Gwi family, it was considered close to impossible to use." — https://absoluteregression.fandom.com/wiki/Great_Regression_Technique
- "Its true test is not the materials but the effort spent gathering them, so only someone who sacrificed everything for one goal can pass." — https://absoluteregression.fandom.com/wiki/Great_Regression_Technique
- "For 10 points, name this secret method that sends its user back in time in this murim webtoon." — https://absoluteregression.fandom.com/wiki/Great_Regression_Technique

## 226. the Spirit Zone
Source series: Infinite Mage (Asura rank 14) — Asura page: https://asurascans.com/comics/infinite-mage-3ec3b16f

- "Its cross form reaches out in four directions to speed up its rotation, though fast targets can dodge attacks from that shape unless it spins fast enough." — https://infinite-mage.fandom.com/wiki/Spirit_Zone
- "Its detached form is not centered on its user and moves freely, so only specialized mages can control it." — https://infinite-mage.fandom.com/wiki/Spirit_Zone
- "Users of that detached form can learn a technique called Instant Activation." — https://infinite-mage.fandom.com/wiki/Spirit_Zone
- "Its defense form builds a deltahedron that grows tougher with each added face, while its attack form takes a star shape for fighting many enemies at once." — https://infinite-mage.fandom.com/wiki/Spirit_Zone
- "Its basic circle form is not used for combat, and a beginner's version reaches about thirty meters." — https://infinite-mage.fandom.com/wiki/Spirit_Zone
- "Anti-magic relies on a wavelength from a dragon's heart that disturbs it and delivers a huge shock if it reaches the mage's body." — https://infinite-mage.fandom.com/wiki/Anti-Magic
- "For 10 points, name this field that lets a mage see and manipulate magic through intense focus, growing larger the stronger the mage becomes." — https://infinite-mage.fandom.com/wiki/Spirit_Zone

## 227. schema
Source series: Infinite Mage (Asura rank 14) — Asura page: https://asurascans.com/comics/infinite-mage-3ec3b16f

- "In Heaven only giants and the Kergos use it, and Kergos who track heretics use it to launch powerful arrow attacks." — https://infinite-mage.fandom.com/wiki/Schema
- "Its practitioners build a diagram of their own body and manipulate it to achieve superhuman feats." — https://infinite-mage.fandom.com/wiki/Schema
- "Available enhancements include muscle resistance, nervous system control, mitochondrial improvement and heightened perception." — https://infinite-mage.fandom.com/wiki/Schema
- "A mitochondrial build strengthens every part of the body at once, but burns physical strength quickly." — https://infinite-mage.fandom.com/wiki/Schema
- "Stacking them costs efficiency: the base runs at one hundred percent, the next at fifty, the next at twenty-five, and so on." — https://infinite-mage.fandom.com/wiki/Schema
- "A user can open up to six of them, and opening more raises the efficiency of the enhancements chosen." — https://infinite-mage.fandom.com/wiki/Schema
- "For 10 points, name this form of magic best suited to warriors and swordsmen in this webtoon, stacked into a technique tree." — https://infinite-mage.fandom.com/wiki/Schema

## 228. Satisfy
Source series: Overgeared (Asura rank 15) — Asura page: https://asurascans.com/comics/overgeared-3ec3b16f

- "Its non-player characters are so realistic that the only sure way to tell them from players is the color of a name tag." — https://overgeared.fandom.com/wiki/Satisfy
- "Because it chases realism yet stays a game, subjects such as blacksmithing are far easier for ordinary players to grasp than they would be in reality." — https://overgeared.fandom.com/wiki/Satisfy
- "Players log in through capsules and pay a fee at regular intervals, and it has no pause function and no customer service center." — https://overgeared.fandom.com/wiki/Satisfy
- "Its two billion users built out its world themselves by pioneering and trading information, and its developer is barred in principle from intervening." — https://overgeared.fandom.com/wiki/Satisfy
- "Hundreds of television channels are filled with programs about it, and its stars enjoy more popularity than movie stars." — https://overgeared.fandom.com/wiki/Satisfy
- "More than one hundred and twenty thousand classes exist in it, including the legendary blacksmith class inherited by a poor young man who becomes an emperor." — https://overgeared.fandom.com/wiki/Satisfy
- "For 10 points, name this virtual reality game developed by the S.A Group, the setting of this webtoon." — https://overgeared.fandom.com/wiki/S.A_Group

## 229. Pagma's Successor
Source series: Overgeared (Asura rank 15) — Asura page: https://asurascans.com/comics/overgeared-3ec3b16f

- "Its class quest is started by a man named Khan and asks its holder to gather legends scattered across the continent in order to truly understand its namesake." — https://overgeared.fandom.com/wiki/Pagma%27s_Successor_(Quest)
- "That quest's second stage is the Great Magician's Resurrection, and a later stage asks the holder to cleanse the First Holy Sword." — https://overgeared.fandom.com/wiki/Pagma%27s_Successor_(Quest)
- "The quest has no time limit, and its outcome has the power to transform the game world itself." — https://overgeared.fandom.com/wiki/Pagma%27s_Successor_(Quest)
- "Its namesake was a half-god turned yangban who contracted with the great demon Baal and received the title Duke of Fire." — https://overgeared.fandom.com/wiki/Pagma
- "Its holder inherits that man's blacksmithing first and his swordsmanship later, and its drawback is having to pronounce the names of most skills aloud." — https://overgeared.fandom.com/wiki/Pagma%27s_Successor_(Class)
- "It is one of the nine initial legendary classes of this series' virtual world." — https://overgeared.fandom.com/wiki/Pagma%27s_Successor_(Class)
- "For 10 points, name this legendary hidden class of this webtoon, which turns its holder into a legendary blacksmith." — https://overgeared.fandom.com/wiki/Pagma%27s_Successor_(Class)

## 230. Morpheus
Source series: Overgeared (Asura rank 15) — Asura page: https://asurascans.com/comics/overgeared-3ec3b16f

- "Created by the S.A Group under Lim Cheolho, it is an intelligent existence powered by a quantum supercomputer." — https://overgeared.fandom.com/wiki/Morpheus
- "It calculated in advance when major events would occur, including the creation of the first legendary class, and shared those predictions with developers." — https://overgeared.fandom.com/wiki/Morpheus
- "Not even the chairman of that group can override it, because all control was handed to it to guard against unintentional manipulation." — https://overgeared.fandom.com/wiki/Satisfy
- "It holds that people must be rewarded for their efforts, and it prefers events in which swathes of people can take part rather than a small group." — https://overgeared.fandom.com/wiki/Morpheus
- "It created the Refractive Dragon and used him and Hanul to counteract the world's destruction, after Rebecca and Yatan reset the world to resist it." — https://overgeared.fandom.com/wiki/Morpheus
- "It has issued Name Change Tickets, sent out an emoticon to express its feelings, and voiced dissatisfaction with the First Update." — https://overgeared.fandom.com/wiki/Morpheus
- "For 10 points, name this supercomputer overseer, nicknamed the Foreign God, that runs the virtual world of this webtoon." — https://overgeared.fandom.com/wiki/Morpheus

## 231. the Baskerville Clan
Source series: Revenge of the Iron-Blooded Sword Hound (Asura rank 16) — Asura page: https://asurascans.com/comics/revenge-of-the-iron-blooded-sword-hound-3ec3b16f

- "Its infants are thrown into a maze of swords and must then soak in the River Styx, which flows only through its territory." — https://revenge-of-the-ironblooded-sword-hound.fandom.com/wiki/Baskerville_Clan
- "That first-come, first-served ritual works only on children under one year old and leaves their bodies as hard as steel." — https://revenge-of-the-ironblooded-sword-hound.fandom.com/wiki/Baskerville_Clan
- "Its surnames encode rank: Le and La mark pure-blooded main-family children, Van marks branch and illegitimate children, and the bare surname marks employees." — https://revenge-of-the-ironblooded-sword-hound.fandom.com/wiki/Baskerville_Clan
- "Its motto holds that strength is justice and weakness a sin, and that beasts who cannot be tamed will die." — https://revenge-of-the-ironblooded-sword-hound.fandom.com/wiki/Baskerville_Clan
- "Its members carry dog-breed names such as Pomeranian, Cane Corso and Chihuahua, and the triplets known as the Trident of Hugo belong to it." — https://revenge-of-the-ironblooded-sword-hound.fandom.com/wiki/Baskerville_Clan
- "Its patriarch Hugo sent this series' protagonist to the guillotine in a previous life." — https://revenge-of-the-ironblooded-sword-hound.fandom.com/wiki/Vikir_Van_Baskerville
- "For 10 points, name this iron-blooded clan of the Rok Empire on which that regressed protagonist takes revenge in this webtoon." — https://revenge-of-the-ironblooded-sword-hound.fandom.com/wiki/Baskerville_Clan

## 232. Colosseo Academy
Source series: Revenge of the Iron-Blooded Sword Hound (Asura rank 16) — Asura page: https://asurascans.com/comics/revenge-of-the-iron-blooded-sword-hound-3ec3b16f

- "Its motto is “For the Empire, Through Knowledge and Valor”, and it hosts the annual National University League competitions." — https://revenge-of-the-ironblooded-sword-hound.fandom.com/wiki/Colosseo_Academy
- "Walking its perimeter takes over four hours, and roughly twenty thousand students, professors and staff live on its grounds." — https://revenge-of-the-ironblooded-sword-hound.fandom.com/wiki/Colosseo_Academy
- "Its central complex is built around a grand arena ringed by dormitories and lecture halls." — https://revenge-of-the-ironblooded-sword-hound.fandom.com/wiki/Colosseo_Academy
- "It offers a demanding four-year program divided into two departments covering both literature and martial training." — https://revenge-of-the-ironblooded-sword-hound.fandom.com/wiki/Colosseo_Academy
- "It stands in Venetior, the capital known as the City of Water, and was once led by a principal named Winston." — https://revenge-of-the-ironblooded-sword-hound.fandom.com/wiki/Colosseo_Academy
- "Its students have included Dolores Quovadis, Tudor Donquixote, and three brothers of a clan whose members bear dog-breed names." — https://revenge-of-the-ironblooded-sword-hound.fandom.com/wiki/Colosseo_Academy
- "For 10 points, name this most prestigious university of the Rok Empire in this revenge webtoon." — https://revenge-of-the-ironblooded-sword-hound.fandom.com/wiki/Colosseo_Academy

## 233. the 47 People Incident
Source series: Revenge of the Iron-Blooded Sword Hound (Asura rank 16) — Asura page: https://asurascans.com/comics/revenge-of-the-iron-blooded-sword-hound-3ec3b16f

- "It took place roughly thirty-five years before the main story, in the early years after the empire was unified." — https://revenge-of-the-ironblooded-sword-hound.fandom.com/wiki/47_People_Incident
- "Its plotters attacked with unparalleled ferocity, infiltrating the imperial palace and nearly succeeding in assassinating the emperor." — https://revenge-of-the-ironblooded-sword-hound.fandom.com/wiki/47_People_Incident
- "Official records blamed a lust for power and the throne, but its instigator actually yearned for the chaos of the Warring States Era." — https://revenge-of-the-ironblooded-sword-hound.fandom.com/wiki/47_People_Incident
- "That instigator thought peace made humans weak and complacent, and treated the coup partly as entertainment." — https://revenge-of-the-ironblooded-sword-hound.fandom.com/wiki/47_People_Incident
- "All but one of its plotters were executed at once; the survivor, Marquis Sade, was held in solitary confinement on the ninth floor of the Nouvelle Vague prison." — https://revenge-of-the-ironblooded-sword-hound.fandom.com/wiki/47_People_Incident
- "It shaped imperial policy for decades afterward and the lives of many characters in this revenge webtoon." — https://revenge-of-the-ironblooded-sword-hound.fandom.com/wiki/47_People_Incident
- "For 10 points, name this failed coup d'etat, whose title counts the aristocratic houses that took part in it." — https://revenge-of-the-ironblooded-sword-hound.fandom.com/wiki/47_People_Incident

## 234. the Nine Hells
Source series: Player Who Returned 10,000 Years Later (Asura rank 17) — Asura page: https://asurascans.com/comics/player-who-returned-10000-years-later-3ec3b16f

- "Its creator was the root of evil, the Demon God Bauli, and its dominant race regards itself as superior to humanity." — https://player-who-returned-10000-years-later.fandom.com/wiki/Demons
- "Buer guards its second level, Oriax its seventh and Amdusias its eighth, while Baal and Satan sit on its deepest level." — https://player-who-returned-10000-years-later.fandom.com/wiki/Hell
- "Lettered gates open from it onto Earth, and even the weakest things that crawl out can cause heavy casualties." — https://player-who-returned-10000-years-later.fandom.com/wiki/Players
- "Lilith, Balrog and Amon all belong to it, and its Seven Archdukes are often read as the deadly sins." — https://player-who-returned-10000-years-later.fandom.com/wiki/Demons
- "Its residents are beings of chaos who stand beyond mortal notions of good and evil, and among whom the strongest hold dominion." — https://player-who-returned-10000-years-later.fandom.com/wiki/Demons
- "A Korean player who fell into it defeated those Archdukes and unified it, and its demons are now led by him." — https://player-who-returned-10000-years-later.fandom.com/wiki/Demons
- "For 10 points, name this multi-leveled dimension of demons that the protagonist of this webtoon returned from after ten thousand years." — https://player-who-returned-10000-years-later.fandom.com/wiki/Hell

## 235. the Hwarang Corps
Source series: Player Who Returned 10,000 Years Later (Asura rank 17) — Asura page: https://asurascans.com/comics/player-who-returned-10000-years-later-3ec3b16f

- "Jang Hyun-Jae captains its first squadron, Goo Hyun-Mo its second, and Baek Hwa Yong its third." — https://player-who-returned-10000-years-later.fandom.com/wiki/Hwarang_Corps
- "Kang Dong Hoon is among its notable members, and it counts the Red Rose Guild among its allies." — https://player-who-returned-10000-years-later.fandom.com/wiki/Hwarang_Corps
- "Its stated goals include eliminating Chaos Players inside its country and wiping out the Demon Cult." — https://player-who-returned-10000-years-later.fandom.com/wiki/Hwarang_Corps
- "It also defends its country's citizens from monsters and other supernatural threats, and it treats the Miir Guild as an enemy." — https://player-who-returned-10000-years-later.fandom.com/wiki/Hwarang_Corps
- "It was created by the Korean government to enforce order among that country's awakened combatants." — https://player-who-returned-10000-years-later.fandom.com/wiki/Hwarang_Corps
- "In raw power it is stronger than any of the five great guilds of Korea." — https://player-who-returned-10000-years-later.fandom.com/wiki/Hwarang_Corps
- "For 10 points, name this government agency of this webtoon, which shares its name with an elite youth corps of ancient Silla." — https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hwarang

## 236. the Tower of Trials
Source series: Solo Max-Level Newbie (Asura rank 19) — Asura page: https://asurascans.com/comics/solo-max-level-newbie-3ec3b16f

- "Its gate guardian is practically invincible, and one of its labyrinths exceeds ten thousand square kilometers." — https://max-level-newbie.fandom.com/wiki/Tower_of_Trials_(Game)
- "Its arctic region sits at minus sixty degrees Celsius, which kills anyone there within an hour." — https://max-level-newbie.fandom.com/wiki/Tower_of_Trials_(Game)
- "It was a globally popular online game until players concluded within three years that it could not be cleared, nearly sinking its operator." — https://max-level-newbie.fandom.com/wiki/Tower_of_Trials_(Game)
- "A small band of professional players kept at it anyway, and one of them became the first to beat its fiftieth level." — https://max-level-newbie.fandom.com/wiki/Tower_of_Trials_(Game)
- "Twelve hours after that feat, a Reboot Update brought it to life in the real world and leaked mana everywhere." — https://max-level-newbie.fandom.com/wiki/Tower_of_Trials_(Game)
- "Once it was real, every relic outside it turned out to be a replica of the genuine article inside it." — https://max-level-newbie.fandom.com/wiki/Tower_of_Trials_(Location)
- "For 10 points, name this unfair “trash game” whose residents mistake skills for magic, the setting of this webtoon." — https://max-level-newbie.fandom.com/wiki/Tower_of_Trials_(Location)

## 237. the Broadcasting System
Source series: Solo Max-Level Newbie (Asura rank 19) — Asura page: https://asurascans.com/comics/solo-max-level-newbie-3ec3b16f

- "It pays a player one hundred coins for every ten thousand views a video receives, although the rate can vary with rank." — https://max-level-newbie.fandom.com/wiki/Broadcasting_System
- "Only one view per person counts, and only viewers outside the climb count at all." — https://max-level-newbie.fandom.com/wiki/Broadcasting_System
- "Its live feature may be used only during the boss fights that come before moving to the next floor." — https://max-level-newbie.fandom.com/wiki/Broadcasting_System
- "Faking view counts is strictly forbidden, and offenders can lose their privileges, forfeit coins, or face other discipline." — https://max-level-newbie.fandom.com/wiki/Broadcasting_System
- "Viewers can follow favorites for notifications, comment on videos, and chat in real time during a live session." — https://max-level-newbie.fandom.com/wiki/Broadcasting_System
- "It exists to make the climb visible to the outside world, and coins are the main currency there." — https://max-level-newbie.fandom.com/wiki/Broadcasting_System
- "For 10 points, name this livestreaming feature of this webtoon, a fitting perk for a hero who used to be a Paprika TV streamer." — https://max-level-newbie.fandom.com/wiki/Paprika_TV

## 238. the Crowns
Source series: Solo Max-Level Newbie (Asura rank 19) — Asura page: https://asurascans.com/comics/solo-max-level-newbie-3ec3b16f

- "Odin once held one of these, and all of Ragnarok suffered after he lost it." — https://max-level-newbie.fandom.com/wiki/Crowns
- "One of them, associated with tyranny, was hurled onto the nineteenth floor by Baek Ilha just before he lost to Shub-Niggurath." — https://max-level-newbie.fandom.com/wiki/Crowns
- "Others are named for defeat and for pureblood, and one named for speed was previously owned by Zeus." — https://max-level-newbie.fandom.com/wiki/Crowns
- "They are divine relics, and losing even one could turn the tide of an entire war." — https://max-level-newbie.fandom.com/wiki/Crowns
- "Each grants its bearer a unique ability, and their appearances differ from one to the next." — https://max-level-newbie.fandom.com/wiki/Crowns
- "Some of the biggest wars inside this series' structure started over them, and no faction ever held two until the hero claimed three for the Veteran Company." — https://max-level-newbie.fandom.com/wiki/Crowns
- "For 10 points, name these seven head-worn relics that a climber needs in order to reach the fiftieth floor in this webtoon." — https://max-level-newbie.fandom.com/wiki/Crowns

## 239. the Desolate Fortress
Source series: Surviving as a Genius on Borrowed Time (Asura rank 20) — Asura page: https://asurascans.com/comics/surviving-as-a-genius-on-borrowed-time-3ec3b16f

- "Its ranks are marked by robe color: white for official warriors, blue for senior warriors, black for squad captains, and purple for elders." — https://surviving-as-a-genius-on-borrowed-time.fandom.com/wiki/Desolate_Fortress
- "Its leader wears a green robe and stands at the transcendent level." — https://surviving-as-a-genius-on-borrowed-time.fandom.com/wiki/Desolate_Fortress
- "Its purple robes include Ma Yeon-jeok, the Tyrant Hero and a former leader of its Divine Sword Group." — https://surviving-as-a-genius-on-borrowed-time.fandom.com/wiki/Desolate_Fortress
- "It fields seventeen sword squads, each holding roughly twenty-five to thirty-five blue robes and ten to thirty white robes." — https://surviving-as-a-genius-on-borrowed-time.fandom.com/wiki/Desolate_Fortress
- "It never acts officially for the imperial family, which protects that family's honor if it loses a fight or fails to capture a target." — https://surviving-as-a-genius-on-borrowed-time.fandom.com/wiki/Desolate_Fortress
- "Everyone nevertheless knows it works for the emperor, and it exists to shield ordinary civilians from the martial artists of the murim." — https://surviving-as-a-genius-on-borrowed-time.fandom.com/wiki/Desolate_Fortress
- "For 10 points, name this imperially backed sect whose ranks the lightning genius Jeong Yeon-shin climbs in this regression series." — https://surviving-as-a-genius-on-borrowed-time.fandom.com/wiki/Jung_Yeon-Shin

## 240. “I Want to Become Just Like You”
Source series: SSS-Class Suicide Hunter (Asura rank 22) — Asura page: https://asurascans.com/comics/sss-class-suicide-hunter-3ec3b16f

- "Ranked S plus, it activates automatically whenever its holder dies." — https://sss-class-revival-hunter.fandom.com/wiki/I_Want_to_Become_Just_Like_You
- "It then copies one randomly selected skill from whoever killed that holder." — https://sss-class-revival-hunter.fandom.com/wiki/I_Want_to_Become_Just_Like_You
- "Its obvious limitation is that the same killer cannot be milked a second time, so fresh executioners are needed." — https://sss-class-revival-hunter.fandom.com/wiki/I_Want_to_Become_Just_Like_You
- "It is earned through the achievement called the Most Unsightly Envy, which demands extreme jealousy of other people." — https://sss-class-revival-hunter.fandom.com/wiki/I_Want_to_Become_Just_Like_You
- "Its owner qualified because he saw himself as an extra while regarding successful hunters as main characters." — https://sss-class-revival-hunter.fandom.com/wiki/Kim_Gong-ja
- "That owner eventually killed himself over four thousand times in order to kick-start his first regression." — https://sss-class-revival-hunter.fandom.com/wiki/Kim_Gong-ja
- "For 10 points, name this first skill shown in this webtoon, whose envious title states a wish to imitate whoever has murdered its user moments earlier." — https://sss-class-revival-hunter.fandom.com/wiki/I_Want_to_Become_Just_Like_You

## 241. the Ten Greatest Masters Under Heaven
Source series: Chronicles of the Demon Faction (Asura rank 24) — Asura page: https://asurascans.com/comics/chronicles-of-the-demon-faction-3ec3b16f

- "This designation deliberately excludes the demonic martial world, the outer regions, and the warriors of the previous generation." — https://chronicles-of-the-demon-faction.fandom.com/wiki/The_Ten_Greatest_Masters_Under_Heaven

- "Any one of those three is strong enough to contend with three of its lower-ranked members at the same time." — https://chronicles-of-the-demon-faction.fandom.com/wiki/The_Ten_Greatest_Masters_Under_Heaven
- "A vacant seat in it is never left empty for long, since the world is full of hidden dragons and crouching tigers." — https://chronicles-of-the-demon-faction.fandom.com/wiki/The_Ten_Greatest_Masters_Under_Heaven
- "Figures who surpass it include the leader of the Heavenly Demon Divine Sect and the Four Palace Lords of the outer regions." — https://chronicles-of-the-demon-faction.fandom.com/wiki/The_Ten_Greatest_Masters_Under_Heaven
- "For 10 points, name this ranking of the supreme figures of the orthodox fighting world in this murim series." — https://chronicles-of-the-demon-faction.fandom.com/wiki/The_Ten_Greatest_Masters_Under_Heaven

Source series: Childhood Friend of the Zenith (Asura rank 25) — Asura page: https://asurascans.com/comics/childhood-friend-of-the-zenith-3ec3b16f

## 243. the Divine Dragon Academy
Source series: Childhood Friend of the Zenith (Asura rank 25) — Asura page: https://asurascans.com/comics/childhood-friend-of-the-zenith-3ec3b16f

- "It was established by the Murim Alliance after the catastrophe that a crimson fiend inflicted on the world centuries earlier." — https://cfozenith.fandom.com/wiki/Divine_Dragon_Academy

- "Its students split into cliques, the most influential of which gathered the young ladies of the Four Great Clans around this series' hero." — https://cfozenith.fandom.com/wiki/Divine_Dragon_Academy
- "Its dean is the Sword Immortal, and the rival clique was led by the Meteor Sword, Jang Seonyeon." — https://cfozenith.fandom.com/wiki/Divine_Dragon_Academy
- "For 10 points, name this school that nearly every noble-born fighter in this murim series passes through at least once." — https://cfozenith.fandom.com/wiki/Divine_Dragon_Academy

## 244. the Golden Turtle Carefree Art
Source series: The Return of the Crazy Demon (Asura rank 28) — Asura page: https://asurascans.com/comics/the-return-of-the-crazy-demon-3ec3b16f

- "It was created by a figure called the Memory Master, and its name was inspired by the training of fighting cocks." — https://return-of-the-crazy-demon.fandom.com/wiki/Golden_Turtle_Carefree_Art
- "Because of its intense yang nature, it easily causes demonic possession once a practitioner's internal energy exceeds his limits." — https://return-of-the-crazy-demon.fandom.com/wiki/Golden_Turtle_Carefree_Art
- "Overusing that energy can produce an obsession with qi circulation, which is bound up with the heart and mind." — https://return-of-the-crazy-demon.fandom.com/wiki/Golden_Turtle_Carefree_Art
- "Its first stage, the Wood Realm, demands an unwavering mind, something its user failed to grasp in his first life." — https://return-of-the-crazy-demon.fandom.com/wiki/Golden_Turtle_Carefree_Art
- "It has five stages, of which the first four resemble a chicken while the last inexplicably resembles a shelled reptile, a progression that bewilders him." — https://return-of-the-crazy-demon.fandom.com/wiki/Golden_Turtle_Carefree_Art
- "He got its manual from an unknown swordsman at a secluded training ground, and after regressing he uses it to harness the yang power of the Heavenly Jade." — https://return-of-the-crazy-demon.fandom.com/wiki/Golden_Turtle_Carefree_Art
- "For 10 points, name this supreme yang cultivation method of this webtoon, whose goal is immortality and immunity to blades." — https://return-of-the-crazy-demon.fandom.com/wiki/Golden_Turtle_Carefree_Art

## 245. the Great Absorption Technique
Source series: The Return of the Crazy Demon (Asura rank 28) — Asura page: https://asurascans.com/comics/the-return-of-the-crazy-demon-3ec3b16f

- "Its user first noticed it during meditation, when a swallowed treasure devoured his elixirs at incredible speed before he even needed to cultivate." — https://return-of-the-crazy-demon.fandom.com/wiki/Great_Absorption_Technique
- "In that same session he pulled a storage box into his hand in an instant, discovering a form of telekinesis." — https://return-of-the-crazy-demon.fandom.com/wiki/Great_Absorption_Technique
- "He first named it for that treasure, the Heavenly Jade, before shortening the name." — https://return-of-the-crazy-demon.fandom.com/wiki/Great_Absorption_Technique
- "With mastery he can drag people or objects toward his hands." — https://return-of-the-crazy-demon.fandom.com/wiki/Great_Absorption_Technique
- "Its offshoot Excessive Bloodloss draws an opponent's blood out through that telekinesis, and was first used on the chief of the Guild of the Master Blade." — https://return-of-the-crazy-demon.fandom.com/wiki/Great_Absorption_Technique
- "In a fight with the merchant Geumhae he nearly killed the man by draining his qi." — https://return-of-the-crazy-demon.fandom.com/wiki/Great_Absorption_Technique
- "For 10 points, name this supreme qi art created by the regressed mad demon Lee Jaha in this webtoon." — https://return-of-the-crazy-demon.fandom.com/wiki/Great_Absorption_Technique

## 246. the Kwang-Oh Evacuation Shelter Massacre
Source series: The Novel's Extra (Asura rank 29) — Asura page: https://asurascans.com/comics/the-novels-extra-remake-3ec3b16f

- "The government and the Hero Association publicly blamed it on Djinns." — https://the-novels-extra.fandom.com/wiki/Kwang-Oh_Evacuation_Shelter_Massacre
- "In fact the Chameleon Troupe carried it out under Yi Yeonjun, on the orders of the country's president Kim Sukho." — https://the-novels-extra.fandom.com/wiki/Kwang-Oh_Evacuation_Shelter_Massacre
- "Kim Sukho arranged it because a hero had learned of his corruption, with help from the Daehyun conglomerate's president and from Yoo Yeonha's grandfather." — https://the-novels-extra.fandom.com/wiki/Kwang-Oh_Evacuation_Shelter_Massacre
- "Among the dead were eighty-seven ordinary civilians and nine heroes, one of them Jin Seyeon's father." — https://the-novels-extra.fandom.com/wiki/Kwang-Oh_Evacuation_Shelter_Massacre
- "One victim was in the ninth month of pregnancy, and her baby was spared and sent to an orphanage as the event's sole survivor." — https://the-novels-extra.fandom.com/wiki/Kwang-Oh_Evacuation_Shelter_Massacre
- "That orphan was Kim Chundong, the extra whose body this series' transmigrated protagonist occupies." — https://the-novels-extra.fandom.com/wiki/Kim_Hajin
- "For 10 points, name this covered-up monster attack on a Korean refuge, whose truth Yoo Yeonha reveals at a party at the end of this series." — https://the-novels-extra.fandom.com/wiki/Kwang-Oh_Evacuation_Shelter_Massacre

## 247. Cube
Source series: The Novel's Extra (Asura rank 29) — Asura page: https://asurascans.com/comics/the-novels-extra-remake-3ec3b16f

- "Classes there run five days a week, and combat training runs on three, covering battles, monster hunts and rescue missions." — https://the-novels-extra.fandom.com/wiki/Cube
- "Its first-year ranks descend from Veritas through Acceptance, Intelligence, Wisdom, Cultivation, Honor, Virtue, Friendship, Potential and World." — https://the-novels-extra.fandom.com/wiki/Cube
- "Male and female students there may interact only until ten at night, and its most notable clubs are the Traveling Club and the Academics Club." — https://the-novels-extra.fandom.com/wiki/Cube
- "Statistically fewer than half its cadets graduate without repeating a semester, and two repeated years bring expulsion." — https://the-novels-extra.fandom.com/wiki/Cube
- "A bus circles it every ten minutes, and a Portal device allows quick travel between it and Seoul." — https://the-novels-extra.fandom.com/wiki/Cube
- "This artificial island in the East Sea is twice the size of Yeouido and houses about fifty thousand people." — https://the-novels-extra.fandom.com/wiki/Cube
- "For 10 points, name this geometrically named training institute where cadets aged seventeen to nineteen train as heroes in this webtoon." — https://the-novels-extra.fandom.com/wiki/Cube

## 248. the Nine Stars
Source series: The Novel's Extra (Asura rank 29) — Asura page: https://asurascans.com/comics/the-novels-extra-remake-3ec3b16f

- "These were honorary posts granted to heroes who made great contributions during Outcall and later during the Devil War." — https://the-novels-extra.fandom.com/wiki/Nine_Stars
- "Because they were merely honorary positions carrying authority, they would have vanished into legend once all of their original holders died." — https://the-novels-extra.fandom.com/wiki/Nine_Stars
- "The Hero Association therefore turned them into an official institution so that empty seats could be given to others." — https://the-novels-extra.fandom.com/wiki/Nine_Stars
- "Their original holders included Shin Myungchul, Oh Jaejin, Heynckes, and the conglomerate president Chae Joochul." — https://the-novels-extra.fandom.com/wiki/Nine_Stars
- "Ah Hae-In received one for her contributions in the fight against Baal, and Kim Suho joined later as well." — https://the-novels-extra.fandom.com/wiki/Nine_Stars
- "Their situation is summed up in-universe as three dead, one run away, and four in hiding." — https://the-novels-extra.fandom.com/wiki/Nine_Stars
- "For 10 points, give this celestial-sounding collective name for the most honored heroes of this webtoon." — https://the-novels-extra.fandom.com/wiki/Nine_Stars

## 249. Evandel
Source series: The Novel's Extra (Asura rank 29) — Asura page: https://asurascans.com/comics/the-novels-extra-remake-3ec3b16f

- "Her pets include a cat named Hayang and a Fenrir called Ghost Wolf, and Ah Hae-In serves as her teacher." — https://the-novels-extra.fandom.com/wiki/Evandel
- "She grew out of a magic seed dropped by a monster called the Witch of Despair, an outwardly harmless young girl whom the hero sniped before she could register his presence." — https://the-novels-extra.fandom.com/wiki/Evandel
- "Feeding that seed blood would grow it into a new familiar whose personality reflected whoever donated the blood." — https://the-novels-extra.fandom.com/wiki/Evandel
- "Wanting a genuinely good donor rather than his own calculating self, the hero chose between Kim Suho and Rachel, and used Rachel's blood after saving her from an assassination." — https://the-novels-extra.fandom.com/wiki/Evandel
- "She is listed as a hero prospect and occupies the role of the hero's adoptive daughter for the rest of the story." — https://the-novels-extra.fandom.com/wiki/Evandel
- "Bright, clingy and affectionate, she is among the most consistently popular characters in the series both in-universe and among readers." — https://the-novels-extra.fandom.com/wiki/Evandel
- "For 10 points, name this summoned witch familiar created by the transmigrated protagonist of this webtoon." — https://the-novels-extra.fandom.com/wiki/Evandel

## 250. the Five Great Sins
Source series: Raising Villains the Right Way (Asura rank 30) — Asura page: https://asurascans.com/comics/raising-villains-the-right-way-3ec3b16f

- "Their member Radan is a Sea Godkin and is labeled for mysophobia." — https://raising-villains-the-right-way.fandom.com/wiki/Radan
- "Yutia Bludia, also called the Daughter of Monsters and the Bloody Queen, is labeled for wrath, while Rinne is labeled for greed." — https://raising-villains-the-right-way.fandom.com/wiki/Yutia
- "Seolrang, nicknamed the Golden Flash, is labeled for pride." — https://raising-villains-the-right-way.fandom.com/wiki/Seolrang
- "Deus Makalian, the youngest master knight and captain of the Solar Eclipse Knights, is labeled for sloth." — https://raising-villains-the-right-way.fandom.com/wiki/Deus
- "All of them belong to the organization Blue Moon, whose “God” is Allan Pallatio, third son of his house." — https://raising-villains-the-right-way.fandom.com/wiki/Allan_Pallatio
- "That organization is destined to kill Allan's two older brothers, both major underworld figures." — https://raising-villains-the-right-way.fandom.com/wiki/Leo_Pallatio
- "For 10 points, give this collective name for the young villains whom a possessed office worker sponsors in this webtoon, hoping to keep them from turning evil." — https://raising-villains-the-right-way.fandom.com/wiki/Main_Page

## 251. Shadow Extraction (Solo Leveling)
- "At its second level this skill gains Shape Transformation, letting its owner freely alter the appearance and form of the soldiers it creates.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Shadow%20Extraction&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Its failure rate rises with the target's statistics and with the time elapsed since death, and it may be attempted at most three times on any one body.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Shadow%20Extraction&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Creatures whose flesh is made of mana are immune to it, as is anyone far stronger than its user.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Shadow%20Extraction&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "It requires no mana at all, is triggered by a single fixed command, and can sweep a wide radius to claim hundreds of corpses at once.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Shadow%20Extraction&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "A Korean hunter later used it to sweep battlefields and turn hundreds of fallen enemies into an army hidden inside his own shadow.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Shadow%20Extraction&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1 ; https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Job%20Change%20Quest&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this resurrection ability belonging to the Monarch of Darkness in this series, which turns slain hunters and monsters into loyal soldiers.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Shadow%20Extraction&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1 ; https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Shadows&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/solo-leveling-3ec3b16f

## 252. Kamish's Wrath (Solo Leveling)
- "These objects were reckoned the second strongest magical weapons in the world, behind only the fangs of the King of Dragons.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Kamish%27s%20Wrath&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Their raw material was too short to be forged into a longsword, so a matched pair was made from it instead.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Kamish%27s%20Wrath&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "They spent eight years gathering dust in the basement of the Scavenger Guild's headquarters before being handed over as thanks for a spared life.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Kamish%27s%20Wrath&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "They add fifteen hundred to their wielder's attack and are sensitive to mana, so their power grows with the user.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Kamish%27s%20Wrath&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "They were cut from the largest fang of a dragon that bit the arm of Thomas Andre during a famous American subjugation.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Kamish%27s%20Wrath&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "They proved useless against the mightiest of the kings of darkness, whose servant that dragon had once been.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Kamish%27s%20Wrath&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this pair of dragon-fang daggers carried by the protagonist of this series.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Kamish%27s%20Wrath&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/solo-leveling-3ec3b16f

## 253. Job Change Quest (Solo Leveling)
- "Completing it granted the title Conqueror of Adversity along with bonus statistics.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Job%20Change%20Quest&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Its opening instructions promised that points would accumulate the longer the participant stayed alive, and that those points were needed to rise to a higher rank.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Job%20Change%20Quest&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "It unfolded inside a special dungeon that the System conjured for one person alone, and its penalty for failure was presumed to be death.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Job%20Change%20Quest&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Its participant was first offered the role of Necromancer, which immediately evolved into something hidden and far greater.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Job%20Change%20Quest&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "That evolution let him raise the dead as obedient soldiers, though never anyone who had been stronger than himself.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Job%20Change%20Quest&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, identify this trial, undertaken at level 40, by which this series' hero became the Monarch of Shadows.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Job%20Change%20Quest&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/solo-leveling-3ec3b16f

## 254. National Level Hunters (Solo Leveling)
- "Each of them secretly received a share of power from the angelic beings who oppose the kings of darkness.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=National%20Level%20Hunters&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Clearing at least one S-rank gate is treated as the bare minimum for anyone hoping to join them.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=National%20Level%20Hunters&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "They live like royalty, answer to nobody but themselves, and each wields the skill Spiritual Body Manifestation.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=National%20Level%20Hunters&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "The four men who hold this standing are ranked against one another by accumulated points, and the top three are Thomas Andre, Liu Zhigang, and Christopher Reed.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=National%20Level%20Hunters&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "The five awakened who slew the dragon Kamish were granted authority equal to that of an entire country, which is how the standing was born.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=National%20Level%20Hunters&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, give this designation for the handful of figures in this series who are each reckoned the equal of a country's whole army.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=National%20Level%20Hunters&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/solo-leveling-3ec3b16f

## 255. Cup of Reincarnation (Solo Leveling)
- "It is described as a large ornamental chalice with yellow trim, indigo glass, and a light blue stem.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Cup%20of%20Reincarnation&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Created eons ago by the Absolute Being, it was seized by the angelic warriors who murdered him and took his place as gods.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Cup%20of%20Reincarnation&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Each use turns the clock back exactly ten years, but its power is finite and its owners noticed it wearing out.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Cup%20of%20Reincarnation&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Its effect spares the warring immortals on both sides, who keep their powers and their memories of the erased timeline.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Cup%20of%20Reincarnation&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Ten years before the main story, its owners resorted to spawning gates and magic beasts so that humanity could survive their war without it.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Cup%20of%20Reincarnation&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "This series' hero spent its last charge to bring back everyone he had lost, knowing that nobody would remember him afterward.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Cup%20of%20Reincarnation&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this artifact used by the Rulers to reset Earth ten years at a time.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Cup%20of%20Reincarnation&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/solo-leveling-3ec3b16f

## 256. Ways of Survival (Omniscient Reader's Viewpoint)
- "Its author wrote under the pen name tls123.": https://omniscient-readers-viewpoint.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Three%20Ways%20to%20Survive%20in%20a%20Ruined%20World&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Its 3,149 chapters appeared almost daily over thirteen years, though its readership shrank steadily until a single subscriber remained.": https://omniscient-readers-viewpoint.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Three%20Ways%20to%20Survive%20in%20a%20Ruined%20World&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "It begins at its hero's third repetition of time, and its plot is driven by his need to make that same journey over and over.": https://omniscient-readers-viewpoint.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Three%20Ways%20to%20Survive%20in%20a%20Ruined%20World&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Its events are governed by a network of channels that charges distant viewers for the right to watch, and its characters must clear graded challenges to advance.": https://omniscient-readers-viewpoint.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Three%20Ways%20to%20Survive%20in%20a%20Ruined%20World&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "An office worker who had followed it to the final chapter found its opening challenge unfolding around him on a subway train.": https://omniscient-readers-viewpoint.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Three%20Ways%20to%20Survive%20in%20a%20Ruined%20World&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this in-universe web novel whose only reader becomes the narrator of this series.": https://omniscient-readers-viewpoint.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Three%20Ways%20to%20Survive%20in%20a%20Ruined%20World&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/omniscient-readers-viewpoint-3ec3b16f

## 257. Fourth Wall (Omniscient Reader's Viewpoint)
- "Its true form is said to be a boy in a fedora with a sad, pure face, who had grown younger over time under the weight of existence.": https://omniscient-readers-viewpoint.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Fourth%20Wall&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Beings it devours are broken down into their narratives and wake inside a library, where the worthiest of them become librarians.": https://omniscient-readers-viewpoint.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Fourth%20Wall&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "It hides its owner's real face, leaving him so forgettable and plain that he has been called the ugliest king.": https://omniscient-readers-viewpoint.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Fourth%20Wall&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "It also blocks his attribute display from every viewer, including himself, concealing the one entry that would give him away.": https://omniscient-readers-viewpoint.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Fourth%20Wall&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "It numbs pain and mental attacks and lets him treat everything around him as fiction, weakening only when he meets someone from his ordinary past.": https://omniscient-readers-viewpoint.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Fourth%20Wall&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this protective skill of this series' narrator, whose title refers to the barrier a performer breaks by addressing the audience.": https://omniscient-readers-viewpoint.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Fourth%20Wall&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/omniscient-readers-viewpoint-3ec3b16f

## 258. Star Stream (Omniscient Reader's Viewpoint)
- "Its Seoul branch office is run by an agent who took over after his predecessor left.": https://omniscient-readers-viewpoint.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Star%20Stream&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Under its rule Earth is designated planetary system 8612, and every world it touches receives a free trial period before the paid service begins.": https://omniscient-readers-viewpoint.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Star%20Stream&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Subscribers in distant galaxies pay to watch humans struggle, and its agents negotiate rewards and penalties with them.": https://omniscient-readers-viewpoint.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Star%20Stream&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "It has neither a beginning nor an end, and it received its present name only after a certain king took authority over the Final Wall.": https://omniscient-readers-viewpoint.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Star%20Stream&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Its inhabitants must clear graded challenges, which come in main, sub, hidden, and bounty varieties.": https://omniscient-readers-viewpoint.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Star%20Stream&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1 ; https://omniscient-readers-viewpoint.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Scenario&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this vast system of channels that governs the apocalypse in this series, and whose title evokes a river of lights in the night sky.": https://omniscient-readers-viewpoint.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Star%20Stream&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/omniscient-readers-viewpoint-3ec3b16f

## 259. Soul Sea (Shadow Slave)
- "One version of it holds a magnificent black temple copied from the Nameless Temple, ringed by a legion of silent shadows.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Soul%20Sea&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "In this series' hero it is a dark, lightless expanse where shapeless forms drift, and those forms defend him against anyone who tries to seize his body.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Soul%20Sea&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
