- "In a companion of his, a calm ocean glows beneath golden clouds with seven suns above it.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Soul%20Sea&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "One of these forms when an aspirant survives a first ordeal and is reborn stronger, faster, and healthier.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Soul%20Sea&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Summoned equipment is dissolved and stored inside it, slowly repairing itself there.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Soul%20Sea&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1 ; https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Memory&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, give the term for this internal reservoir that stores summoned equipment and reflects its owner's cores.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Soul%20Sea&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/shadow-slave-3ec3b16f

## 260. First Nightmare (Shadow Slave)
- "Its monster, an awakened tyrant over four meters tall, has five milky white eyes and four arms, two of which end in bone claws.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Sunny%27s%20First%20Nightmare&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "That creature turns the corpses of those it kills into dormant beasts known as its larvae.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Sunny%27s%20First%20Nightmare&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "While it was under way, rocks and heavy shards of ice rained down on the participants.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Sunny%27s%20First%20Nightmare&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "This series' hero entered his in the body of a shackled temple slave whose skin markings marked him as the property of a forgotten god.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Sunny%27s%20First%20Nightmare&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "He came out of it with a final appraisal of Glorious and the true name Lost from Light.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Sunny%27s%20First%20Nightmare&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Anyone the magical infection has claimed is dragged into one of these after a week of fighting off the urge to sleep.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Sunny%27s%20First%20Nightmare&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1 ; https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Nightmare%20Spell&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, give the term for this opening ordeal that every sleeper in this series must survive.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Sunny%27s%20First%20Nightmare&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/shadow-slave-3ec3b16f

## 261. Aspect (Shadow Slave)
- "Mastering the legacy attached to one of these earns a relic powerful enough that whole clans have been founded around a single such item.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Aspect&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Every rank its carrier gains adds another ability tied to it, with dormant, awakened, and ascended tiers giving way to a transformation at the highest level.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Aspect&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Unlike almost everything else in its setting, it belongs to the person rather than to the magic that grants it.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Aspect&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "They are usually sorted into combat and utility kinds, the latter useless in a fight yet prized by society.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Aspect&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Every one of them is unique to its holder, though relatives often awaken similar ones.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Aspect&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Whoever receives one also receives a Flaw, a personal curse that is the price of magical power.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Aspect&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this magical specialization that this series' sleepers gain after surviving their first ordeal.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Aspect&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/shadow-slave-3ec3b16f

## 262. Antarctica Campaign (Shadow Slave)
- "It was prompted by irregularities in the Obel Scale appearing for a second time, and the government spent two years preparing for it.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Antarctica%20Campaign&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Unlike the earlier crisis it involved no single gargantuan portal, but a chain reaction of hundreds of smaller ones.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Antarctica%20Campaign&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Thousands of Awakened crossed the Dream Realm in a quiet exodus while their bodies were shipped to ports and sealed inside sleeping pods.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Antarctica%20Campaign&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Members of the First Evacuation Army sheltered in better-defended citadels, some run by the government and others impregnable legacy strongholds such as Bastion and Ravenheart.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Antarctica%20Campaign&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "The chain of portals it confronted also spread the magical infection to unusually many teenagers, and even to people past the usual age.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Antarctica%20Campaign&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this vast military operation in this series, fought on the coldest continent on Earth.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Antarctica%20Campaign&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/shadow-slave-3ec3b16f

## 263. Sky Eating Water (Trash of the Count's Family)
- "Absorbing the lake of the dragon Maxillienne made it three times stronger and left four black droplets tattooed on each side of its owner's collarbone.": https://lout-of-counts-family.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Sky%20Eating%20Water&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "It had rested in Grey Eye Lake before this series' hero claimed it as the sixth of his hidden gifts.": https://lout-of-counts-family.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Sky%20Eating%20Water&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Its original holder was born a slave and freed by the God of War, who then bound her with a title and a duty she had never chosen.": https://lout-of-counts-family.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Sky%20Eating%20Water&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "After the Sekka household drove her from the Paerun Kingdom, that god took back the river she had made.": https://lout-of-counts-family.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Sky%20Eating%20Water&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "When its owner traveled to the Central Plains, this was the gift that was sealed most heavily, which marked it as his most destructive.": https://lout-of-counts-family.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Sky%20Eating%20Water&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "It lets him raise pillars and hurl spears made of liquid.": https://lout-of-counts-family.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Sky%20Eating%20Water&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this water-based hidden gift that raises liquid pillars and spears for its owner.": https://lout-of-counts-family.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Sky%20Eating%20Water&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/trash-of-the-counts-family-3ec3b16f

## 264. Awakened Academy (Shadow Slave)
- "Its induction ceremonies are held monthly early in the year and then weekly as the winter solstice approaches, because the rate of infection is hopelessly chaotic.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Awakened%20Academy&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "It stands in the Siege Capital of the Northern Quadrant and is the only government-funded school of its kind on Earth.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Awakened%20Academy&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Built as a city within a city, it has a high alloy wall, a deep moat, and heavy turrets arranged to create a deadly air-suppression dome.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Awakened%20Academy&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Roughly a thousand young people a year pass through it, receiving food, lodging, and a wide choice of preparatory classes.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Awakened%20Academy&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Each of its dormitory rooms has an adjoining meditation space and an individual sleeping pod.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Awakened%20Academy&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this state-run institution where this series' sleepers are trained before their first descent into the ruined otherworld.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Awakened%20Academy&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/shadow-slave-3ec3b16f

## 265. soulbound weapon (Shadow Slave)
- "Designing one requires three separate tasks: forging the item, binding it, and choosing its enchantments.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Soulbound%20Weapon&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For the one he built, this series' hero found mystical ice shards in the Brilliant Emporium because they suited a radiant lineage descended from a sun god.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Soulbound%20Weapon&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "He could copy nearly every enchantment of the Mantle of the Underworld except the one naming its bearer a prince.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Soulbound%20Weapon&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "He also considered the Unseen enchantment of the Moonlight Shard, which makes a ghostly stiletto appear with no delay at all.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Soulbound%20Weapon&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "He resolved instead to design new enchantments, treating the job as a watershed in his growth as a sorcerer.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Soulbound%20Weapon&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "To tailor it properly he had to watch its recipient's true fighting art rather than the imitation she used to deceive her clan.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Soulbound%20Weapon&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, give the term for such an item in this series, a summonable memory tied so closely to its owner that it is exactly as strong as she is.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Soulbound%20Weapon&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1 ; https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Memory&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/shadow-slave-3ec3b16f

## 266. Ancient Powers (Trash of the Count's Family)
- "Human-made examples of these, such as those devised by Nelan Barrow, are weaker than the ones whose affinity came straight from nature.": https://lout-of-counts-family.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Ancient%20Power&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "When their holder dies, they come to rest inside a special object or place until someone else claims them.": https://lout-of-counts-family.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Ancient%20Power&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Collecting more than one usually kills a person horribly, since a human body cannot bear the strain of several at once.": https://lout-of-counts-family.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Ancient%20Power&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "To hold several of differing attributes safely, one must gather all five natural elements, earth, fire, wind, wood, and water, and so reach a state of balance.": https://lout-of-counts-family.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Ancient%20Power&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "They become complete only when a soul fully absorbs them, and this series' hero still hears the voices of his because he refuses to swallow their wills.": https://lout-of-counts-family.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Ancient%20Power&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, give the term for these hidden gifts from a bygone age, each won by overcoming a trial.": https://lout-of-counts-family.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Ancient%20Power&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/trash-of-the-counts-family-3ec3b16f

## 267. Super Rock (Trash of the Count's Family)
- "Its original holder found a hidden path to the Eastern continent that only monsters could pass, which mutated them as they crossed, and he sealed it with his own strength.": https://lout-of-counts-family.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Super%20Rock&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "That holder, orphaned young, was a legendary guardian whose stated dream was three hearty meals a day and sleep without worry.": https://lout-of-counts-family.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Super%20Rock&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "He raised a grand villa deep in the Forest of Darkness so that his friends' successors would not arrive at a shabby place.": https://lout-of-counts-family.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Super%20Rock&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "This series' hero acquired it in that villa simply by eating a pebble.": https://lout-of-counts-family.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Super%20Rock&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "It raises spears of earth from the ground and makes its user's body unbreakable, but only while he is shielding someone else.": https://lout-of-counts-family.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Super%20Rock&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "He first used it at the Gorge of Death, willing to die to save two children from a berserk half-dragon.": https://lout-of-counts-family.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Super%20Rock&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this earth-based defensive gift that becomes unbreakable while its owner shields someone else.": https://lout-of-counts-family.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Super%20Rock&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/trash-of-the-counts-family-3ec3b16f

## 268. dead mana (Trash of the Count's Family)
- "The alchemists' towers of the Mogoru Empire experimented with it on people, killed people to harvest it, and built a bomb out of it.": https://lout-of-counts-family.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Dead%20Mana&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Rei Stecker amputated his own hand after it was tainted by this substance.": https://lout-of-counts-family.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Dead%20Mana&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "A human who survives absorbing it may become either a necromancer or a black mage, though the pain never leaves him afterward.": https://lout-of-counts-family.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Dead%20Mana&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "It appears as a gas or as an easily evaporated black liquid.": https://lout-of-counts-family.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Dead%20Mana&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Races with the darkness attribute use it to work magic, while it poisons everyone else, dyeing them black as they die.": https://lout-of-counts-family.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Dead%20Mana&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "This series' hero passed a small amount of a dragon's version of it to a certain crown prince, multiplying that prince's strength several times over.": https://lout-of-counts-family.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Dead%20Mana&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, give the term for this poisonous magical energy, produced by creatures that are no longer alive.": https://lout-of-counts-family.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Dead%20Mana&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/trash-of-the-counts-family-3ec3b16f

## 269. Dominating Aura (Trash of the Count's Family)
- "It is the one thing the White Crown fears, and controlling that artifact requires it.": https://lout-of-counts-family.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Dominating%20Aura&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Its bearer obtained it from the Black Bone Dragon in a swamp inside the Forest of Darkness, having previously belonged to Nelan Barrow.": https://lout-of-counts-family.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Dominating%20Aura&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "An elder dragon judged it the equal of a grown dragon's Fear, and its presence reminded one whale princess of her father, the Whale King.": https://lout-of-counts-family.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Dominating%20Aura&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "It does nothing but radiate terror, which makes it useless against a true warrior yet perfect for the confidence tricks its owner prefers.": https://lout-of-counts-family.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Dominating%20Aura&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Its own voice told him that to master someone is to take everything from him, even his breath, and that fear is the easiest way to do it.": https://lout-of-counts-family.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Dominating%20Aura&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this hidden gift whose terror can force weaker beings to submit without a fight.": https://lout-of-counts-family.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Dominating%20Aura&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/trash-of-the-counts-family-3ec3b16f

## 270. Order of Vigilance (The Tutorial Is Too Hard)
- "Its founder demonstrated that instant death still works during the Day of Great Harmony even though ordinary injuries heal, and the first execution followed at once.": https://the-tutorial-is-too-hard.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Order%20of%20Vigilance&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "It was created in the third round, and its commander, Park Jung-ah, handles all of its paperwork.": https://the-tutorial-is-too-hard.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Order%20of%20Vigilance&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Kim Min-hyuk, its captain for the normal setting, is widely regarded as the group's real leader.": https://the-tutorial-is-too-hard.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Order%20of%20Vigilance&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Its strike divisions exist to step in first and contain unexpected trouble during large gatherings such as tournaments.": https://the-tutorial-is-too-hard.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Order%20of%20Vigilance&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Its rules forbid violence against another person, theft, and any violation of another's freedom.": https://the-tutorial-is-too-hard.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Order%20of%20Vigilance&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this organization that enforced rules and contained trouble inside the hundred-floor tutorial.": https://the-tutorial-is-too-hard.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Order%20of%20Vigilance&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/the-tutorial-is-too-hard-3ec3b16f

## 271. Relic Syndrome (Tomb Raider King)
- "A cure for it can be produced from the synergy between Qin Shi Huang's elixir plant and the treasure of Xufu.": https://tomb-raider-king.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Relic%20Syndrome&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Patients left untreated for a long time end up infertile.": https://tomb-raider-king.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Relic%20Syndrome&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Its stranger forms include soul swapping and self-harm, while its physical forms run from bleeding and infection to organ failure and pneumonia.": https://tomb-raider-king.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Relic%20Syndrome&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Its mental forms include hallucinations, withdrawal into one's room, and coma.": https://tomb-raider-king.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Relic%20Syndrome&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "It strikes people exposed for too long to the living treasures of this series, especially those the treasures hold in contempt for leaning on affinity.": https://tomb-raider-king.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Relic%20Syndrome&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1 ; https://tomb-raider-king.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Relics&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "A global organization concealed information about it for more than fifteen years, and exposing that secret helped bring the organization down.": https://tomb-raider-king.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Relic%20Syndrome&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1 ; https://tomb-raider-king.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Pandora&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this occupational illness of treasure hunters, caused by handling those treasures for too long.": https://tomb-raider-king.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Relic%20Syndrome&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/tomb-raider-kin-g-3ec3b16f

## 272. Pandora (Tomb Raider King)
- "Its executive board had access to Odin's throne Hlidskjalf, Loki's relic, and an entity called Un-Known.": https://tomb-raider-king.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Pandora&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Its headquarters, modeled on the Burj Khalifa and named for Odin's hall, stands in Manhattan.": https://tomb-raider-king.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Pandora&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Made up of European and North American states, it was originally meant to gather artifacts and hand them over to monopolists.": https://tomb-raider-king.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Pandora&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "The United States eventually broke away from it after absorbing more than half of its high-grade users.": https://tomb-raider-king.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Pandora&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Roughly eighty percent of its members were exposed as corrupt and arrested thanks to this series' hero, and it was finally dismantled altogether.": https://tomb-raider-king.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Pandora&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this global organization that monopolized relics before its corruption was exposed.": https://tomb-raider-king.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Pandora&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/tomb-raider-kin-g-3ec3b16f

## 273. tombs (Tomb Raider King)
- "Their walls bear a sacred script that only this series' hero can read, which discloses each entrance, its traps, and the identity of its master.": https://tomb-raider-king.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Tombs&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "An unwritten rule holds that whoever survives by killing everyone else takes the prize inside.": https://tomb-raider-king.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Tombs&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "The fastest route to that prize is the one with the most traps, since a shorter path always carries more of them.": https://tomb-raider-king.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Tombs&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "They may lie underground, underwater, in the air, or in another dimension entirely, and their entrances range from a cave to a public restroom.": https://tomb-raider-king.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Tombs&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Each is the only place where its owner can use its full power, and their real purpose is to kill and torment as many people as possible.": https://tomb-raider-king.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Tombs&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, give the term for these trap-filled sites where relics awaken and torment intruders.": https://tomb-raider-king.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Tombs&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/tomb-raider-kin-g-3ec3b16f

## 274. Twelve Zodiacs (Return of the Disaster-Class Hero)
- "This series' protagonist sorts most of them into tiers he calls Toxic Waste, Parasite, and Fishbone, according to how much he dislikes each one.": https://return-of-the-disaster-class-hero.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Twelve%20Zodiacs&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Their number includes Heiji, who contracted with Freyja and Freyr, and Kevin Hazard, who contracted with Artemis.": https://return-of-the-disaster-class-hero.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Twelve%20Zodiacs&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Yang Wei's patron among them is the Jade Emperor, while Hugo Otters answers to Apollo.": https://return-of-the-disaster-class-hero.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Twelve%20Zodiacs&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Each is a divine saint bound to a god matching his or her sign, and there is no clear representative among the thirteen original awakened.": https://return-of-the-disaster-class-hero.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Twelve%20Zodiacs&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "The protagonist himself holds the hidden thirteenth seat, that of the serpent bearer.": https://return-of-the-disaster-class-hero.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Twelve%20Zodiacs&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this group of contracted Saints who each answer to a god of the ecliptic.": https://return-of-the-disaster-class-hero.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Twelve%20Zodiacs&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/return-of-the-disaster-class-hero-3ec3b16f

## 275. Battle of the Black Skull (Shadow Slave)
- "Madoc died in it at the hands of a creature that came out of a category four portal.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Battle%20of%20the%20Black%20Skull&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "It ended when the armies of both great clans and of the government were trapped between nightmare portals, three of which were of that same category.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Battle%20of%20the%20Black%20Skull&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Each of the thousands of portals in its region connects to a Seed of Nightmare surrounding the prize at its center.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Battle%20of%20the%20Black%20Skull&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Both sides used the Call to move Saints, Masters, and Awakened into forts and temporary outposts, then pushed expeditions toward a dark pyramid.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Battle%20of%20the%20Black%20Skull&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Its combatants, the Song and Valor clans, were racing to claim the Tomb of Ariel and the citadel guarding it.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Battle%20of%20the%20Black%20Skull&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "It was fought in East Antarctica alongside the larger government operation there.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Battle%20of%20the%20Black%20Skull&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this clan war in this series, called after the citadel that both sides raced to seize.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Battle%20of%20the%20Black%20Skull&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/shadow-slave-3ec3b16f

## 276. Hamel Style (Damn Reincarnation)
- "Its ninth form, Infinite Purgatory, gathers sword-force into a sphere said to be able to erase a mountain.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Hamel%20Style&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Its seventh, Dead End, is a decapitating downward swing, while its first, Mana Parrying, astonished even the archwizard Sienna.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Hamel%20Style&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Its signature trick, Ignition, wrings out extra power by straining the user's body and cores, shortening his life a little each time.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Hamel%20Style&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Vermouth Lionheart compiled the whole set after its inventor's death and handed it to his own second son.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Hamel%20Style&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "That son became the first leader of the Black Lions, whose descendants practice it to this day.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Hamel%20Style&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Its inventor now cringes whenever those names are shouted aloud, since he chose them himself three centuries ago.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Hamel%20Style&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this family of sword techniques in this series, called after the dead mercenary who devised them.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Hamel%20Style&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/damn-reincarnation-3ec3b16f

## 277. Demon Kings (Damn Reincarnation)
- "The weakest of them, associated with carnage, was the first to fall to the subjugation party three centuries ago.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Demon%20Kings&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "The one associated with fury was defeated by Agaroth along with his adopted children, and then bargained to be reborn with his memories intact and his power preserved beneath the sea.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Demon%20Kings&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "The second strongest, associated with imprisonment, had once led a party sworn to destroy all of them, and took his throne only to seal a traitor and reshape the world.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Demon%20Kings&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "That traitor became the strongest of them, associated with destruction, and lost his sense of self before devouring the world.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Demon%20Kings&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Five of these existed, and in ancient times even a human could ascend to their station through countless sacrifices.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Demon%20Kings&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, give the shared title held by the five supreme fiends who ruled Helmuth.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Demon%20Kings&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/damn-reincarnation-3ec3b16f

## 278. Five Great Heroes (Damn Reincarnation)
- "Its wisest member was an orphan abandoned in an elven forest who learned magic from the elves who raised her.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Five%20Great%20Heroes&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Its faithful member was a saintess of the Holy Empire, while its bravest was a tribal chief's son and the first recruit.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Five%20Great%20Heroes&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Its founder had been a slave awaiting sacrifice who stole a sword from his captors and led the other survivors out.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Five%20Great%20Heroes&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Its last member, known to everyone as the Stupid one, was a village boy turned mercenary who joined only after losing duel after duel to that founder.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Five%20Great%20Heroes&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "This band led subjugation forces sent by every kingdom and killed three demon sovereigns in their own castles.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Five%20Great%20Heroes&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this legendary company of champions, whose foul-mouthed swordsman is reborn three centuries later into his old comrade's noble house.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Five%20Great%20Heroes&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/damn-reincarnation-3ec3b16f

## 279. Moonlight Sword (Damn Reincarnation)
- "It was found in a dungeon beneath the castle of the demon lord of carnage, and neither its maker nor its earlier owner is known.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Moonlight%20Sword&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Its blade bears no carving at all and gives off a soft glow, which it can still emit and even solidify when the blade is missing.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Moonlight%20Sword&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "It played the largest part in killing two of the five demon lords.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Moonlight%20Sword&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Judging it too dangerous for anyone to hold, its owner shattered it after making peace and left only its hilt to an old comrade.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Moonlight%20Sword&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Even that hilt cancels spells and cuts through every kind of energy, and its heir has slowly rebuilt the weapon from scattered shards.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Moonlight%20Sword&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "It was eventually revealed to belong to the mightiest demon lord, whose blood alone lets anyone wield it.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Moonlight%20Sword&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this divine blade that killed two demon lords before its owner shattered it.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Moonlight%20Sword&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/damn-reincarnation-3ec3b16f

## 280. Helmuth (Damn Reincarnation)
- "Its citizens pay their monthly taxes not in money but in easily recovered life force, which they scarcely miss.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Devildom%20of%20Helmuth&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Vast numbers of demonic beasts bound as familiars, along with undead raised by black wizards, perform all of its difficult labor.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Devildom%20of%20Helmuth&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "As a result its fields are covered in golden wheat whatever the season, and every citizen is guaranteed a living even without working.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Devildom%20of%20Helmuth&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Three hundred years ago it was a lawless place that was fatal to anyone who was not a demon.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Devildom%20of%20Helmuth&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "An oath sworn between a human champion and its imprisoning sovereign turned it into a startlingly welcoming country.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Devildom%20of%20Helmuth&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this homeland of the demon race in this series, whose comfortable welfare state astonishes the reborn hero who once invaded it.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Devildom%20of%20Helmuth&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/damn-reincarnation-3ec3b16f

## 281. Akasha (Damn Reincarnation)
- "It and a staff called Vladimir are the only two of their kind made from a dragon's heart.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Akasha&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "That heart came from a dragon who died with the name of its eventual owner on his lips, and it was enchanted with a surviving dragon's blessing.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Akasha&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Like the Holy Sword it chooses its own bearer, and for the two centuries after its owner vanished it refused every high-ranking magician who tried.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Akasha&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Unlike that sword, however, its owner may deliberately hand ownership to someone else.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Akasha&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "It sharply magnifies a user's grasp of magical formulas, letting its new wielder learn spells far faster.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Akasha&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "It belonged to the wisest of the champions who slew the demon lords, a woman raised among elves.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Akasha&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1 ; https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Five%20Great%20Heroes&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this legendary staff of this series, which shares its name with a Sanskrit word for ether or sky.": https://damn-reincarnation.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Akasha&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/damn-reincarnation-3ec3b16f

## 282. Other World (Reincarnator)
- "In the first timeline it served as a twenty-five-year training ground for the invasion it was meant to prepare humanity against.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=The%20Otherworld&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Its colorless first region gives newcomers roughly three months free of interference from experienced adventurers.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=The%20Otherworld&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "At the end of that period the survivors must gather materials and build ships to sail onward.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=The%20Otherworld&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Its seven main regions are ordered like a rainbow, from red through violet, each holding more energy than the last.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=The%20Otherworld&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Travel between them happens by finding a dimensional portal and jumping through, and nobody can ever return to a region already left.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=The%20Otherworld&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this one-way dimension of seven coloured regions that humanity must cross.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=The%20Otherworld&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/reincarnator-3ec3b16f

## 283. Five Calamities (Reincarnator)
- "One of them, a serpent coiled around a trunk, harms nobody and merely drinks the poison fed to its host.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Five%20Calamities&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Another draws toxins in through an inside mouth and spews them out through an outside mouth, fueling the growth of every beast on that host.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Five%20Calamities&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "A third swims the sea eating the growing roots of that host, and its breathing alone raises waves and tornadoes.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Five%20Calamities&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "A cult called the Quadratus worships them, and each is powered by a fragment of a divine stone lodged in its heart.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Five%20Calamities&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "They were engineered from demonic creatures by the elven natives of the red region, and a body-stealing schemer used them to weaken the tree sheltering his rivals.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Five%20Calamities&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1 ; https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Seven%20Legacies&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Because they are meant to be endured or evaded rather than slain, they act as natural disasters for anyone crossing that region.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Five%20Calamities&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this quintet of monstrous scourges gnawing at a world tree.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Five%20Calamities&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/reincarnator-3ec3b16f

## 284. Numbering (Reincarnator)
- "Items in this category that keep absorbing runes and growing are nicknamed the Draconic Series, and their standing is fixed only once they reach the violet stage.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Numbering&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "The Manoros Clan created it by judging the 999 strongest skills and artifacts across the seven regions.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Numbering&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Its top nine entries are called solo, the next ninety double, and the remaining nine hundred triple.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Numbering&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Entries at the zero level are also called Skills of Annihilation, and a single one of them can wipe out an entire world.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Numbering&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Those skills can be earned only by the transcendent, and holding several of them shatters the soul of anyone who is not.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Numbering&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, give the term for this ranking of the 999 strongest artifacts and skills across seven regions.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Numbering&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/reincarnator-3ec3b16f

## 285. Seven Legacies (Reincarnator)
- "The one in the indigo region is a red jade flame that powers the half-biological armor grown on a moon in the blue region.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Seven%20Legacies&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "The green region's consists of a blessing from the Ains that must then be filled by a spirit, since the blessing alone is an empty house.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Seven%20Legacies&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "The orange region's is a body enhancement surgery the Akarons devised using the genes of lava-drinking creatures.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Seven%20Legacies&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "The red region's is a colossal tree whose branches teleport, whose fruit speeds growth, and whose roots purify poison.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Seven%20Legacies&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "A race called the Napa planted each of them to prepare for their own revival, seeding hosts for their waiting souls.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Seven%20Legacies&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, give the collective name for these inheritances of vanished races, one left in each colored region, which this series' hero gathers to strengthen humanity.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Seven%20Legacies&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/reincarnator-3ec3b16f

## 286. Great Collapse (Reincarnator)
- "Light runs along the metal columns it brings, and with a loud grinding sound each of them cracks open into a doorway.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=The%20Abyss&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Those columns descend from the sky and settle onto holes that have opened in the ground and the air and then stabilized.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=The%20Abyss&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "It begins with a tremor that everyone on the planet feels at once, after which the heavens split and mountain ranges break apart.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=The%20Abyss&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "It marks the opening of the endless pit whose residents then pour through those doorways.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=The%20Abyss&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "In the first timeline it killed everyone who had not yet reached the violet region.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=The%20Abyss&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this apocalypse in which metal columns open as gates and the Abyss pours into the world.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=The%20Abyss&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/reincarnator-3ec3b16f

## 287. Immortal Soul (Reincarnator)
- "Its mastery advances only when its user's body is torn apart and his very self feels close to destruction, so it grows fastest amid varied punishment.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Immortal%20Soul&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Mastering it turns its holder into a transcendent, and its regeneration then matches that of a deathless being.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Immortal%20Soul&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "This series' hero won it in the green region as a reward from a fairy for killing Tiamet, and it pairs well with his Demonic Dragon Reinforcement.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Immortal%20Soul&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "It was rated the single strongest ability known to humanity, holding the top place in the solo tier.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Immortal%20Soul&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "It earned that place partly through the exploits of Kim Kangtae, the strongest human, who charged the front line trusting it to keep healing him.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Immortal%20Soul&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "It regrows tissue, mends shattered bodies, and restores lost limbs, letting its user fight with no fear of injury.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Immortal%20Soul&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this top-ranked regeneration skill that restores tissue, shattered bodies, and lost limbs.": https://reincarnator.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Immortal%20Soul&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/reincarnator-3ec3b16f

## 288. ARK-2020 (Murim Login)
- "A hidden final page of its manual could be read by one user alone, who found it after logging out for the first time.": https://murimlogin.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=ARK-2020&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "That page promised a capsule bound to a single player until death, sweeping manipulation of time, and a synchronization system between user and character.": https://murimlogin.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=ARK-2020&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "One character argues that its apparent slowing of time is an illusion, since a brain can hold only so much data at once.": https://murimlogin.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=ARK-2020&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Made by H-SOFT and released on Victory Day, it lets skills learned inside it carry over into the real world.": https://murimlogin.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=ARK-2020&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "New users cannot log out until they reach a sufficient level, and dying inside it kills the body outside.": https://murimlogin.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=ARK-2020&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this virtual-reality capsule that traps a bottom-ranked hunter in a martial-arts world.": https://murimlogin.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=ARK-2020&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1 ; https://murimlogin.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Jin%20Taekyung&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/murim-login-3ec3b16f

## 289. shadow soldiers (Solo Leveling)
- "Those of general grade and above can speak, but only in the tongue of monsters, which most humans cannot understand.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Shadows&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Grade also decides personality: the lowest have almost none, knight-ranked ones manage simple emotions, and marshals have fully formed characters, one of them fond of Korean period dramas.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Shadows&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "They regenerate so completely that not even an S-rank can destroy them, though one dragon's breath proved able to end them for good.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Shadows&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "At their master's discretion they can hide inside a living person's own silhouette, watching everything and signaling him across any distance.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Shadows&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "They grow steadily stronger by killing monsters, and they stay fiercely loyal even to a master who was their enemy in life.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Shadows&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "This series' hero raises them from the corpses of hunters and beasts, and they extended the same loyalty to his son.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Shadows&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1 ; https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Shadow%20Extraction&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, give the term for these undead troops commanded by the Monarch of Darkness.": https://solo-leveling.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Shadows&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/solo-leveling-3ec3b16f

## 290. Black Obelisk (Shadow Slave)
- "After the River People fled the Tomb of Ariel, it was ceded to them and became the heart of a floating settlement.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Black%20Obelisk&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Under the blessing of Ananke of Weave, its spire turned silver, wrapped in strands of woven silk that hold up vast floating platforms.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Black%20Obelisk&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "What looks like a gargantuan stone spire rising from the water is really the tip of a finger.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Black%20Obelisk&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Below the surface lie four more fingers on an immense hand crusted with barnacles and seaweed, and deeper still an arm leading to a colossal head.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Black%20Obelisk&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Divers refuse to go past that head, since anyone who sees its face dies or goes mad.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Black%20Obelisk&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Standing in unusually calm water near the shores of the Stormsea, it is the citadel closest to the mouth of the River of Tears.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Black%20Obelisk&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this citadel of this series, whose dark stone pillar resembles an Egyptian monument.": https://shadowslave.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Black%20Obelisk&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/shadow-slave-3ec3b16f

## 291. Crack Museum (Regressor Instruction Manual)
- "Its guide is an ego ring named Marx, the last surviving member of the guardians who built it.": https://regressor-instruction-manual.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Crack%20Museum&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Among its three mythic-grade holdings are a god's hammer and Durendal, listed as the sword of the twelfth knights.": https://regressor-instruction-manual.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Crack%20Museum&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Its legendary shelves include a Dragon Lord's Parenting Set and a Doom Beast's gauntlets.": https://regressor-instruction-manual.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Crack%20Museum&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Exactly thirty explorers may enter it at once, and on entering they receive a compulsory tour quest.": https://regressor-instruction-manual.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Crack%20Museum&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Once inside, wearing that guide's ring, they cannot leave until the tour is finished or a price is paid.": https://regressor-instruction-manual.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Crack%20Museum&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Sealed creatures, contracted beings, and visitors from other dimensions are all kept there alongside its weapons and equipment.": https://regressor-instruction-manual.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Crack%20Museum&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this unrated dungeon entered thirty explorers at a time under a compulsory tour quest.": https://regressor-instruction-manual.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Crack%20Museum&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/regressor-instruction-manual-3ec3b16f

## 292. Continental Management Committee (Regressor Instruction Manual)
- "Its warp points, built and maintained by Jung Hayan and Han Sora, serve as teleportation hubs across the land.": https://regressor-instruction-manual.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Continental%20Management%20Committee&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Its roster grew from Lee Jihye, Jung Hayan, and Cha Heera to include deities such as Belial and Benigore, plus ordinary salaried staff.": https://regressor-instruction-manual.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Continental%20Management%20Committee&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Kim Hyunsung is listed among those with access to it, though he does not know that.": https://regressor-instruction-manual.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Continental%20Management%20Committee&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "It occupies an unknown location that mortals can enter yet where gods can also reside comfortably.": https://regressor-instruction-manual.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Continental%20Management%20Committee&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Inside, it resembles a modern office, with private rooms, paperwork, and a famously lighthearted atmosphere.": https://regressor-instruction-manual.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Continental%20Management%20Committee&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Lee Kiyoung founded it to administer everything from air and water to dungeons and magic, so that the land would not fall apart.": https://regressor-instruction-manual.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Continental%20Management%20Committee&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this bureaucratic body founded by this series' regressor to keep an entire landmass running.": https://regressor-instruction-manual.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Continental%20Management%20Committee&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/regressor-instruction-manual-3ec3b16f

## 293. Winterbottom Kit (Children of the Rune)
- "Its English-derived name describes someone who lived in a valley only in winter while spending the summer on upland pastures.": https://children-of-the-rune.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Winterbottom%20Kit&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Its armored half is a silver chainmail piece covering only the chest, called Snowguard.": https://children-of-the-rune.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Winterbottom%20Kit&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1 ; https://children-of-the-rune.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Snowguard&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Its other half is an outsized sword named for the coldest season, wielded by a teenage boy whose skill rivals a seasoned mercenary's.": https://children-of-the-rune.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Winterbottom%20Kit&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1 ; https://children-of-the-rune.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Boris%20Jineman&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Both of its pieces work ice magic, and both are described as carriers of a soul.": https://children-of-the-rune.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Winterbottom%20Kit&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "The two together are the heirloom of a noble family of the Travaches Republic that fell when the master of its manor was betrayed by his younger brother.": https://children-of-the-rune.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Winterbottom%20Kit&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1 ; https://children-of-the-rune.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=House%20of%20Jineman&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this pair of magical treasures in this series, consisting of a winter blade and a white breastplate.": https://children-of-the-rune.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Winterbottom%20Kit&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/children-of-the-rune-3ec3b16f

## 294. Jineman (Children of the Rune)
- "Its butler Tulkh is also a magician, and a dog named Malorie lives on its estate.": https://children-of-the-rune.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=House%20of%20Jineman&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Yevgnen, born in 966 of the local era, and Boris, born eight years later, are among its children.": https://children-of-the-rune.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=House%20of%20Jineman&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Izenia, its lady, died in the same year that Boris was born.": https://children-of-the-rune.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=House%20of%20Jineman&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "In the year 986 the master of its manor, Yulken, was brought down by his own younger brother Vlado.": https://children-of-the-rune.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=House%20of%20Jineman&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "It is a noble house of the Travaches Republic, and its heirloom is a matched pair of ice-magic treasures.": https://children-of-the-rune.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=House%20of%20Jineman&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1 ; https://children-of-the-rune.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Winterbottom%20Kit&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this noble family of this series, whose surviving younger son wanders the world carrying a winter sword.": https://children-of-the-rune.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=House%20of%20Jineman&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1 ; https://children-of-the-rune.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Boris%20Jineman&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/children-of-the-rune-3ec3b16f

## 295. Hell difficulty (The Tutorial Is Too Hard)
- "On its fourth floor the monsters are orcish goblins two meters tall, where the gentlest option offers only mushroom men.": https://the-tutorial-is-too-hard.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Hell%20Difficulty&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1 ; https://the-tutorial-is-too-hard.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=The%20tutorial&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Between those extremes, one option sends goblins as people imagine them, and another sends goblins as they actually are.": https://the-tutorial-is-too-hard.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Hell%20Difficulty&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1 ; https://the-tutorial-is-too-hard.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=The%20tutorial&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Every option is presented in a floating question box that first asks whether a person wishes to take part at all.": https://the-tutorial-is-too-hard.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Hell%20Difficulty&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1 ; https://the-tutorial-is-too-hard.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=The%20tutorial&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Greater rewards are promised at this level, since the hundred floors of the tower scale with the choice made at the start.": https://the-tutorial-is-too-hard.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Hell%20Difficulty&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1 ; https://the-tutorial-is-too-hard.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=The%20tutorial&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "A professional gamer drowning his sorrows in drink picked it out of sheer pride when he was dragged into that tower.": https://the-tutorial-is-too-hard.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Hell%20Difficulty&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this hardest setting of this series' training tower, which shares its name with the traditional place of eternal punishment.": https://the-tutorial-is-too-hard.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Hell%20Difficulty&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/the-tutorial-is-too-hard-3ec3b16f

## 296. Talaria's Wings (The Tutorial Is Too Hard)
- "Its upgraded form granted its user the title of the man who might become the apostle of a ditzy deity.": https://the-tutorial-is-too-hard.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Talaria%27s%20Wings&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "In its first version it was made hastily, allowing only gliding, though its two halves already had enormous magic defense.": https://the-tutorial-is-too-hard.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Talaria%27s%20Wings&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "It draws entirely on its creator's power, so it costs the caster no mana, no concentration, and no divine energy.": https://the-tutorial-is-too-hard.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Talaria%27s%20Wings&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "After improvement it granted sustained flight, faster mana recovery for allies in range, ten extra points of strength, and colors that shift at the caster's will.": https://the-tutorial-is-too-hard.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Talaria%27s%20Wings&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Enemies weak to holy power shrivel in fear when it appears.": https://the-tutorial-is-too-hard.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Talaria%27s%20Wings&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "The God of Adventures created it out of sheer impatience as a gift to a challenger who had caught his interest.": https://the-tutorial-is-too-hard.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Talaria%27s%20Wings&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this flight skill of this series, named for the sandals of Hermes.": https://the-tutorial-is-too-hard.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Talaria%27s%20Wings&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/the-tutorial-is-too-hard-3ec3b16f

## 297. Vintage Clock (Kidnapped Dragons)
- "Its aliases include the White Bird and the Keeper of Providence, and it communicates chiefly through text emoticons.": https://kidnappeddragons.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Vintage%20Clock&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Cheerful but envious, it is said to resent the timepiece that its partner received as a gift.": https://kidnappeddragons.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Vintage%20Clock&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "It is one of seven godly authorities, and it is the one that guards the providence of time.": https://kidnappeddragons.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Vintage%20Clock&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "It can sever the flow of time itself, and it rewinds the world whenever its partner dies or the planet enters an apocalypse.": https://kidnappeddragons.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Vintage%20Clock&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Through every iteration from the first to the seventh it has stood beside Yu Jitae and taught him what he knows.": https://kidnappeddragons.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Vintage%20Clock&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this time-manipulating Authority that resets the world whenever its partner dies.": https://kidnappeddragons.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Vintage%20Clock&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/kidnapped-dragons-3ec3b16f

## 298. Forest of Roots (Helmut: The Forsaken Child)
- "Its five regions are each held by a demonic beast, and four of them echo the auspicious animals of Chinese myth: a serpent, a colorful bird, a tiger, and a turtle.": https://helmut-the-forsaken-child.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Forest%20of%20Roots&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "The fifth, a leopard called Elaga, has no clear mythic counterpart, and its appearance accompanies both an abandoned heir's arrival and a Sword Saint's death.": https://helmut-the-forsaken-child.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Forest%20of%20Roots&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "A barrier surrounds it that no demonic beast can cross, although its air is thick with demonic energy.": https://helmut-the-forsaken-child.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Forest%20of%20Roots&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Criminals are dropped into it by divine magic, and anyone who walks out again has his sins forgiven.": https://helmut-the-forsaken-child.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Forest%20of%20Roots&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "The church later used it to dispose of people it found inconvenient, since nobody had ever come back.": https://helmut-the-forsaken-child.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Forest%20of%20Roots&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this monster-filled place of exile from which no condemned person was expected to return.": https://helmut-the-forsaken-child.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Forest%20of%20Roots&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/helmut-the-forsaken-child-3ec3b16f

## 299. Bloody Shadows Guild (Reaper of the Drifting Moon)
- "Its second leader was Gu Juyang, and its enforcers were ranked as First through Seventh Sword.": https://reaper-of-the-drifting-moon.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Bloody%20Shadows%20Guild&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Its methods copied an old poison craft in which creatures were sealed together in a jar until only the strongest remained.": https://reaper-of-the-drifting-moon.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Bloody%20Shadows%20Guild&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "It abducted three hundred children and kept each of them alone in a tiny dark cell.": https://reaper-of-the-drifting-moon.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Bloody%20Shadows%20Guild&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Those children were then released into a mansion and deliberately underfed, so that they killed one another over food.": https://reaper-of-the-drifting-moon.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Bloody%20Shadows%20Guild&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Survivors were beaten and dosed with addictive techniques, leaving them disposable puppets for its missions.": https://reaper-of-the-drifting-moon.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Bloody%20Shadows%20Guild&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "This series' assassin protagonist is listed among those three hundred children.": https://reaper-of-the-drifting-moon.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Bloody%20Shadows%20Guild&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this assassins' organization that raised three hundred abducted children through lethal competition.": https://reaper-of-the-drifting-moon.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=Bloody%20Shadows%20Guild&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/reaper-of-the-drifting-moon-3ec3b16f

## 300. Garden of Sunset (Everyone Else is a Returnee)
- "Its founder concealed himself so well that the Heaven's Army, which should have had jurisdiction over his world, learned nothing about him beyond that fact.": https://everyone-else-is-a-returnee.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=The%20Garden%20of%20Sunset&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Unlike the Destruction Demon Army or the Army of Brilliant Light, it never opposed anyone openly, which one character found more unsettling than open hostility.": https://everyone-else-is-a-returnee.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=The%20Garden%20of%20Sunset&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Its members are higher existences who claim they can read the future, prompting that same character to call it a cult.": https://everyone-else-is-a-returnee.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=The%20Garden%20of%20Sunset&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "It expanded slowly but surely until its strength matched the other great factions, coming and going like the wind.": https://everyone-else-is-a-returnee.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=The%20Garden%20of%20Sunset&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "Its leader turned out to be the archangel Gabriel, who eventually became a true dragon.": https://everyone-else-is-a-returnee.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=The%20Garden%20of%20Sunset&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- "For 10 points, name this secretive faction whose leader was revealed as the archangel Gabriel.": https://everyone-else-is-a-returnee.fandom.com/api.php?action=parse&page=The%20Garden%20of%20Sunset&prop=wikitext&format=json&redirects=1
- Series carried by Asura Scans: https://asurascans.com/comics/everyone-else-is-a-returnee-3ec3b16f
